    SELECT TOP(1) [e].[EmployeeId], [e].[Department], [e].[Email], [e].[FirstName], [e].[LastName], [e].[ManagerId], [e].[PasswordHash], [e].[RoleId], [e].[Salary], [e].[UserName], [e0].[EmployeeId] AS [EmployeeId0], [e0].[Department] AS [Department0], [e0].[Email] AS [Email0], [e0].[FirstName] AS [FirstName0], [e0].[LastName] AS [LastName0], [e0].[ManagerId] AS [ManagerId0], [e0].[PasswordHash] AS [PasswordHash0], [e0].[RoleId] AS [RoleId0], [e0].[Salary] AS [Salary0], [e0].[UserName] AS [UserName0], [r].[RoleId] AS [RoleId1], [r].[Name]
    FROM [Employees] AS [e]
    LEFT JOIN [Employees] AS [e0] ON [e].[ManagerId] = [e0].[EmployeeId]
    INNER JOIN [Roles] AS [r] ON [e].[RoleId] = [r].[RoleId]
    WHERE [e].[EmployeeId] = @__employeeId_0
) AS [s]
LEFT JOIN [Leaves] AS [l] ON [s].[EmployeeId] = [l].[EmployeeId]
ORDER BY [s].[EmployeeId], [s].[EmployeeId0], [s].[RoleId1]
